*Alexandre G. Leclercq,Noémie N. Moreau,Hugo Audebert,Andros Nassar,Thomas Cochin,Thomas Leleu,Loïc Le Henaff,Alexis Desmonts,Yoann Poirier,Aurélie Dubru,Laura Guillemette,Pascal Lecoeur,Kévin Lemasson,Cyril Jaudet,Sébastien Bougleux,Romain Hérault,Carole Brunaud,Samuel Valable,Dinu Stefan,Charlotte Raboutet,Alain Batalla,Joëlle Lacroix,Roman Rouzier,Aurélien Corroyer-Dulmont*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CFB-GBM v2.0数据集，这是先前CFB-GBM数据集的扩展版本，包含264名接受标准Stupp方案治疗的胶质母细胞瘤（GBM）患者。该版本的主要贡献是将全时间点（t0、t1、t2）的肿瘤总体积（GTV）勾画完成率从35%提升至97%，并基于这些纵向量化勾画衍生出RANO 2.0反应分类标签，同时提供脑掩膜、影像组学特征和WHO分类指南信息，已在TCIA上公开发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质母细胞瘤是最具侵袭性的成人原发性脑肿瘤，中位总生存期仅为15个月。开发可复现的计算方法用于治疗反应预测、疾病进展建模和个性化医疗，亟需包含全面临床和治疗数据的纵向多模态影像数据集。此前CFB-GBM数据集的GTV勾画覆盖率有限（35%），限制了其在下游分析中的完整应用。

Method: 使用在BraTS 2021上预训练并在CFB-GBM真实轮廓上微调的nnU-Net模型，完成所有时间点（t0、t1、t2）的GTV勾画，将完成率从35%提升到97%，并由五位放射肿瘤科医生验证。基于纵向GTV标注衍生出所有时间点对的RANO 2.0反应分类标签。此外，使用HD-BET计算脑掩膜，使用PyRadiomics提取影像组学特征，并记录每位患者适用的WHO分类指南（2016版 vs 2021版）。

Result: CFB-GBM v2.0成功将GTV勾画完成率提升至97%，提供了完整的纵向RANO 2.0反应分类标签、脑掩膜和预计算的影像组学特征等辅助数据，并在TCIA上公开发布。

Conclusion: CFB-GBM v2.0作为一个更完整、更易用且可复现的纵向GBM多模态数据集，为治疗反应预测、疾病进展建模和个性化医疗等计算方法的开发提供了更坚实的数据基础，有望推动GBM精准医学研究的进展。

Abstract: Glioblastoma (GBM) is the most aggressive primary brain tumor in adults, with a median overall survival of 15 months. Longitudinal, multi-modal imaging datasets with comprehensive clinical and treatment data are essential to support the development of reproducible computational methods for treatment response prediction, disease progression modelling, and personalized medicine. We present CFB-GBM v2.0, an extension of our previously released CFB-GBM dataset comprising 264 GBM patients treated according to the standard Stupp protocol. The primary contribution of this release is the completion of Gross Tumour Volume (GTV) delineations across all available timepoints ($t_0$, $t_1$ and $t_2$), increasing the overall GTV completion rate from 35% to 97%. This was achieved using a nnU-Net model pre-trained on BraTS 2021 and fine-tuned on CFB-GBM ground-truth contours, with the generated segmentations validated by five radiation oncologists. From these longitudinal GTV annotations, volumetric RANO 2.0 response category labels were derived for all available temporality pairs ($t_0 \rightarrow t_1$, $t_0 \rightarrow t_2$ and $t_1 \rightarrow t_2$). To further ease dataset usability and reproducibility, brain masks computed with HD-BET and pre-computed radiomic features extracted with PyRadiomics are provided for each patient timepoint and MRI modality. Additionally, the WHO classification guideline (2016 vs. 2021) applicable to each patient's diagnosis is now explicitly documented. CFB-GBM v2.0 is publicly available on The Cancer Imaging Archive (TCIA) at https://www.cancerimagingarchive.net/collection/cfb-gbm .

</details>


### [73] [Comparative Study of Out-of-the-Box Technology for Automatic Target Detection and Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17917)
*Alma M. Liezenga,Lotte Nijskens,Henrik R. Baumann,Stefan Becker,Simon Bensberg,Niccolò Camarlinghi,Håvard R. Eiring,Alexander W. Johnsgaard,Tanel Liiv,Giuseppe Martino,Matteo Marturini,Matthias Rapp,Jan Erik van Woerden,Alexander Wolpert,Hugo J. Kuijf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索使用公开模型和民用数据集实现军事场景下的自动目标检测与识别（ATD/R）。作者在新获取的军事数据集上对YOLO系列和DETR框架的多个模型进行了基准测试，验证了预训练版本和在VisDrone数据集上微调后的版本，评估了A2G和G2G视角、目标尺寸和模型尺寸对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 军事ATD/R系统对决策支持和自主操作至关重要，但公开可用的军事数据集稀缺，限制了先进目标检测模型在该领域的应用。作者希望探索如何使用公开模型和民用数据集在军事场景中获得合理性能。

Method: 在新获取的军事相关数据集（包含军事车辆、遮挡和中小目标）上，对YOLO系列的六个版本和DETR框架的两个变体进行基准测试。每个模型分别验证其开箱即用版本和经过VisDrone数据集（包含小目标、空对地视角和相关类别）微调的版本，使用mAP@0.5和mAP@0.5:0.95指标进行评估。

Result: 主要发现：（1）更大的模型优于更小的模型；（2）DETR-based模型相比YOLO系列展现出有前景的结果；（3）在域外A2G数据集上微调可提升A2G性能并略微改善小目标检测；（4）所有模型在A2G场景下检测小目标仍存在困难。

Conclusion: 尽管目标检测领域近期取得了显著进展，但领域内训练对于构建有效的ATD/R系统仍然至关重要。仅依赖公开模型和民用数据集进行迁移学习不足以完全满足军事场景的需求。

Abstract: Automatic Target Detection and Recognition (ATD/R) is critical for military decision support and (semi-)autonomous operations. Recent advances in object detection and artificial intelligence (AI) significantly boosted the potential performance of ATD/R. However, the scarcity of publicly available military datasets limits the application of these systems. As a solution, this paper explores the use of publicly available models and civilian datasets to achieve reasonable performance in military contexts. We benchmark several state-of-the-art models, including six iterations of the YOLO series and two variations on the DETR framework, on a newly acquired military relevant dataset. This dataset features military vehicles and challenging circumstances, including various degrees of occlusions and small targets. The out-of-the-box version of each model is validated alongside a version finetuned on the VisDrone dataset. This dataset features small objects, an Air-to-Ground (A2G) perspective and relevant classes, potentially generalizing to our military ATD/R task. We compare the performance of the models using mAP@0.5 and mAP@0.5:0.95, across A2G and Ground-to-Ground (G2G) perspective, target size and model size, giving insight into the real-time capabilities of models. Our main findings are: (1) bigger models outperform smaller models, (2) DETR-based models show promising results compared to the YOLO series,(3) fine-tuning models on an out-of-domain A2G dataset, improves their A2G performance and slightly improves their performance on small objects, but (4) all models still struggle with detecting small objects in an A2G scenario. We conclude that, despite recent advances in object detection, in-domain training is still crucial for creating capable ATD/R systems.

</details>


### [74] [AppendiGrade: An XAI-Enhanced Deep Learning Framework for Grading Appendicitis in Ultrasound with Gaussian Blur and Grad-CAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17923)
*Fahad Ahammed,Omar Faruq Shikdar,Navid Zaman,Md Tahsin,Md. Nawab Yousuf Ali,Golam Sorwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超声图像的自动检测复杂阑尾炎的系统，使用4679张5类超声图像和四种预训练深度学习模型（DenseNet201、InceptionV3、ConvNextTiny、VGG19）进行分类。经过图像预处理、超参数调优、模型微调和图像锐化等优化后，InceptionV3的准确率从69.21%提升至95.58%，并使用Grad-CAM生成热力图辅助专家交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂性阑尾炎（如穿孔或脓肿形成）与单纯性阑尾炎的准确鉴别是临床难题，而超声作为一种无辐射、成本更低的诊断手段，需要借助自动化系统提高诊断准确性和效率。

Method: 使用4679张超声图像（包含穿孔、脓肿、急性、阑尾粪石和正常5类），分别训练DenseNet201、InceptionV3、ConvNextTiny和VGG19四种预训练深度学习模型；在初始性能不佳后，应用图像预处理、超参数调优、模型微调和图像锐化等优化技术提升性能，并通过Grad-CAM进行模型可解释性分析。

Result: InceptionV3在初始配置下准确率为69.21%（排名第二），经过优化后准确率显著提升至95.58%；Grad-CAM热力图可清晰展示模型预测所依赖的感染区域。

Conclusion: 优化的深度学习系统能够高精度地从超声图像中自动检测复杂阑尾炎，Grad-CAM的可视化输出有助于临床专家进行交叉验证，提升诊断的可靠性和实用性。

Abstract: Appendicitis is one of the most common abdominal emergencies worldwide and requires prompt diagnosis and treatment to prevent life-threatening conditions. However, accurately differentiating complicated cases, such as perforation or abscess formation, from uncomplicated appendicitis remains a significant clinical challenge. Among other methods, ultrasound is a safer and more cost-efficient diagnostic technique because of the lack of radiation exposure. In this research, an advanced system capable of automatically detecting complicated appendicitis from ultrasound images was developed. A dataset consisting of 4679 ultrasound images with 5 classes, namely perforated, abscess, acute, appendicolith, and normal, was used for the proposed model training and testing. Four pretrained deep learning models, DenseNet201, InceptionV3, ConvNextTiny, and VGG19, have been employed for detecting and classifying complicated appendicitis. In the initial configuration, InceptionV3 achieved the second highest accuracy, with a value of 69.21%. Owing to suboptimal performance with raw images, further optimization techniques, including image preprocessing, hyperparameter tuning, model fine-tuning, and image sharpening, were applied. These enhancements significantly improved the model's performance, with an accuracy of 95.58% for InceptionV3. The model performance is then explained with gradient-weighted class activation mapping (Grad-CAM), which creates a heatmap of the regions responsible for the model's prediction of the infected areas. This could make crosschecking with experts much easier.

</details>


### [75] [PerFact: Perception-Derived Fact Prompting for 3D Brain MRI Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17926)
*Jianyu Sun,Zhenxuan Zhang,Guang Yang,Peter J. Lally*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在3D多序列脑部MRI（多疾病容积影像）上重新审视放射学报告生成的假设，发现模型规模并非决定报告质量的关键因素。作者提出PerFact方法，将上游3D分割和分类的感知输出序列化为结构化事实语句，注入到LoRA适配的视觉-语言模型中生成报告。实验表明感知派生事实优于检索到的既往报告，检索在有事实存在时变得多余，预测事实在推理时无需真实标注仍然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告生成几乎完全在2D胸部X光片上发展成熟，默认路线是使用更大骨干网络或在医疗数据上预训练。本文质疑这一假设在3D多序列脑部MRI（容积多疾病场景）下是否成立，并探究真正决定报告质量的主导可控因素。

Method: PerFact方法：将感知任务委托给上游3D分割和分类模型，将其输出序列化为结构化事实语句，然后提示经LoRA适配的视觉-语言模型生成报告。在固定骨干网络、数据划分、目标报告和适配方式的受控研究中，仅改变注入的接地信息（grounding），比较感知派生事实与检索的既往报告的效果。

Result: 零样本医学和放射学视觉-语言模型迁移到脑部MRI效果差，胸部X光专家模型表现最差。五个骨干网络在三个模型家族和一个数量级规模范围内微调后差异微小。感知派生事实优于检索的既往报告；检索在有事实时变得多余；端到端预测事实在无真实标注推理下仍有效。预测事实与真实事实之间的残余差距由事实粒度而非生成器解释。封闭式视觉问答对报告质量无显著损害。

Conclusion: 在3D脑部MRI上，注入的接地信息（grounding information）而非模型选择是报告质量的主导可控因素。这一发现挑战了放射学报告生成领域将模型规模视为关键杠杆的传统假设。

Abstract: Radiology report generation has matured almost entirely on 2D chest radiographs, where the default route to better reports is a larger backbone or a pre-training one on medical data. We revisit that assumption on 3D multi-sequence brain MRI, a volumetric multi-disease regime, and find that the model is not the lever. Zero-shot medical and radiology vision-language models transfer poorly to brain MRI, with chest radiograph specialists failing most conspicuously, and five backbones fine-tuned identically across three model families and an order of magnitude in scale differ only marginally. What determines the quality of the report is the information injected into the prompt. We delegate perception to upstream 3D segmentation and classification, serialize their outputs into a structured fact sentence, and prompt a LoRA-adapted vision-language model with it; we call this \textbf{PerFact}. In a controlled study that fixes the backbone, data split, target reports, and adaptation while varying only the injected grounding, perception-derived facts outperform retrieved prior reports, retrieval becomes redundant once facts are present, and end-to-end predicted facts remain effective without any ground-truth annotation at inference. The residual gap between predicted and oracle facts is explained by the granularity of the facts rather than by the generator. Closed-ended visual question answering comes at no measurable cost to report quality, though the grounding source has little effect on it. On 3D brain MRI, grounding information, not model choice, is the dominant controllable factor in report quality.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond Instrument Motion: Recognizing Tissue Tension Toward Surgical Skill Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17935)
*Marko Haralovi,Zhiqi Miao,Alexander Machiel Bont,Jiapan Guo,Frans van Workum,Estefania Talavera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了组织张力识别这一新的手术视频理解任务，构建了首个专家标注的组织张力数据集SurgTension，并提出了基于稀疏点轨迹的轻量级框架TensionTRAC，在性能上可与强预训练视频骨干网络竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术性能评估主要依赖人工专家评审，耗时、主观且难以规模化。现有手术视频理解方法（如器械分割、手术阶段识别、动作识别）未能显式捕捉细粒度的组织处理质量，而组织张力是手术质量的关键指标。为此，作者提出组织张力识别这一新任务来填补空白。

Method: 1) 提出组织张力识别新任务，针对腹腔镜和机器人辅助直肠癌手术；2) 构建SurgTension数据集，首个专家标注的组织张力基准数据集；3) 提出TensionTRAC框架，一种基于稀疏点轨迹的轻量级方法，通过紧凑的轨迹编码器建模组织张力。

Result: TensionTRAC在组织张力识别任务上取得了与强预训练视频骨干网络相竞争的性能，同时保持轻量级架构优势。

Conclusion: 本文为手术视频理解领域引入了组织张力识别这一新任务和数据集，并验证了基于轨迹建模的轻量级方法在捕捉细粒度组织处理质量上的有效性，为客观、可扩展的手术质量评估提供了新方向。

Abstract: Surgical performance assessment in minimally invasive surgery largely relies on manual expert review, making it time-consuming, subjective, and difficult to scale. While existing surgical video understanding methods address tasks such as instrument segmentation, surgical phase recognition, and action recognition, they do not explicitly capture fine-grained tissue handling, a key indicator of surgical quality. To address this gap, we introduce tissue tension recognition, a new clinically motivated video understanding task for laparoscopic and robot-assisted rectal cancer surgery. To support this task, we construct SurgTension, the first expert-annotated tissue tension dataset, providing a benchmark for objective tissue tension recognition. We further propose TensionTRAC, a lightweight trajectory-based framework that models tissue tension from sparse point trajectories. Using a compact trajectory encoder, TensionTRAC achieves competitive performance against strong pretrained video backbones.

</details>


### [77] [Cross-Domain Generalization in Machine Unlearning via Label-Conditioned Energy Magnitude Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17942)
*Syed Ali Ahmed,Syed Bilal Ahsan,Muhammad Zaigham Zaheer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究机器遗忘中概念遗忘对模型其余部分的影响，提出基于标签条件能量模型（EBM）的遗忘方法，通过能量提升、保留锚定、全局边距、能量正则化及基于DINOv2相似度的传播项实现类级遗忘，实验表明遗忘不仅作用于目标类，还会传播至相似样本，且不牺牲其他类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法大多将遗忘概念视为孤立处理，忽略了遗忘操作对模型其余部分的影响。作者希望从能量模型视角直接观察遗忘效果，探究遗忘一个类别时模型其他部分会发生什么变化。

Method: 基于标签条件EBM为每个类别分配能量，通过遗忘项提升目标类图像-标签对的能量实现遗忘；同时引入保留锚定项维持预训练模型行为、全局边距项、能量正则化项防止能量无界增长，以及传播项基于DINOv2相似度将遗忘信号作用于相似保留样本。

Result: 在DomainNet子集上遗忘sketch域类别可同时消除real、clipart、painting域的对应类别，遗忘错误率达98%-99%并传播至最相似类别；在CIFAR-10上逐一遗忘十类均达100%遗忘率，其余九类平均保留98.5%的原有准确率。

Conclusion: 遗忘不是孤立操作，遗忘信号会通过相似性传播到相关样本，且该方法能在实现完全遗忘的同时最大限度保留其他类别的性能，为机器遗忘研究提供了能量模型视角的新理解。

Abstract: Machine unlearning removes the influence of specific data from a trained model. However, most methods treat the forgotten concept as isolated. In this paper, we study what happens to the rest of the model when a class is forgotten, using a label-conditioned energy-based model (EBM) that assigns per-class energies, making the effect directly observable. We forget a class by raising the energy of its image-label pairs, training with a forget term, a retain anchor to the pretrained model, a global margin, and an energy regularizer that stops the energy magnitudes from growing without limit. A propagation term applies the same forget signal to retain samples, weighted by each sample's DINOv2 similarity to the forget class, so forgetting reaches images that resemble it and leaves the rest untouched. We evaluate on two benchmark datasets: 1) On a subset of DomainNet across four visual domains, we forget tiger, lion, and scissors one at a time. Forgetting a class in the sketch domain also erases it from real, clipart, and painting, with forgetting error reaching 98% and 99% for lion and scissors, and the effect carrying over to the most similar class. 2) On CIFAR-10, we turn off the propagation term and forget each of the ten classes on its own. Forgetting is complete (100%), while the other nine classes retain 98.5% of their pre-unlearning accuracy on average.

</details>


### [78] [SFMformer: A Spatial-Frequency Modulation Transformer for Lightweight Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17966)
*Chih-Hsiang Yang,Chia-Min Lin,Ching-Yu Tsai,Yung-Che Wang,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SFMformer，基于稀疏注意力中"选择质量"与"聚合质量"可分离的观察，在注意力前后分别加入双分支空间增强与小波域调制，使模型在15个基准-尺度对上增益呈现超加性，最终在30项PSNR/SSIM指标中28项排名第一，且参数量低于一百万。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力图像超分方法仅关注整体网络改进，但稀疏化后网络存在两个独立的质量瓶颈：token选择阶段与特征聚合阶段。被选择阶段丢弃的token无法在下游恢复，因此需要分别优化这两个阶段，而非笼统地改进网络。

Method: 在渐进式聚焦注意力的输入端加入双分支空间增强模块提升选择质量，在输出端加入小波域调制模块提升聚合质量，二者组合构成SFMformer。同时采用每block一次而非每layer一次的频谱调制策略以降低计算成本。

Result: 联合增益在9对基准上超过两个模块单独增益之和；增益差异符号与较弱模块的独立贡献呈r=-0.72的负相关，说明两模块在缓解不同约束时互补、缓解同一约束时重叠。频谱调制频率降到六分之一仍保持效果，模型各尺度均低于1M参数，在30项PSNR/SSIM中28项排名第一，并成功在Raspberry Pi 5上部署。

Conclusion: 稀疏注意力网络中，选择与聚合是两个可分离的优化目标，分别置于注意力前后的模块能产生超加性增益；同时论文诚实报告了配对无效的情况，并验证了设计在资源受限设备上的实用性。

Abstract: Sparse attention mechanisms, which score all token pairs but propagate only the strongest, now underpin the most efficient Transformers for lightweight image super-resolution. This paper observes that sparsification changes what it means to improve such a network. A dense attention layer has one place where representation quality matters: the aggregation of attended features. A sparse layer has two, because the top-k operator first decides which tokens survive and only then decides what to do with them, and a token discarded at the selection stage cannot be recovered downstream. Selection quality and aggregation quality are therefore separable targets, addressed by modules placed before and after the attention respectively. We test this by pairing a dual-branch spatial enhancement on the input of a progressive focused attention with a wavelet-domain modulation on its output, forming SFMformer. Measuring each module alone and jointly over all fifteen benchmark-scale pairs, we find their gains are not additive: the joint gain exceeds the sum of the individual gains on nine pairs, and the sign of the discrepancy is predicted by how much the weaker module contributes on its own (r = -0.72), so the two compound when they relieve different constraints and overlap when they relieve the same one. Enabling spectral modulation once per block rather than once per layer retains the effect at roughly one-sixth of its cost, keeping the model below one million parameters at every scale. SFMformer ranks first on 28 of 30 PSNR/SSIM entries across five benchmarks and three upscaling factors. We report the cases where the pairing does not help, and deploy the model on a Raspberry Pi 5 to confirm the design is practical under tight resource budgets.

</details>


### [79] [LinCa: Accelerating Diffusion Models via Learnable Decomposed Feature Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17973)
*Jinshan Liu,Haoran Qin,Xiaobing Tu,Jiacheng Liu,Jiahui Hu,Zhengan Yan,Yukun Xie,Kerui Shen,Jinkui Ren,Yuqi Lin,Xiantao Zhang,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LinCa是一个基于可学习可逆网络的特征缓存框架，通过将缓存特征分解为具有不同连续性的子组件并应用差异化预测策略，在仅增加不到0.2%参数的情况下实现5-7倍加速，同时在FLUX、Qwen-Image和HunyuanVideo等模型上保持近乎无损的图像/视频生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像和视频生成中表现出色，但迭代采样的高计算成本是实际部署的关键瓶颈。虽然特征缓存已成为一种有前景的加速方案，但现有无需训练的方法采用统一的预测策略，无法适应异构的特征动态，在高加速比下导致严重的质量退化。

Method: LinCa提出统一的"分解-预测-重建"（Decompose-Predict-Reconstruct）流程：通过轻量级可逆网络将缓存特征分解为具有不同连续性属性的子组件，为每个子组件匹配差异化的预测顺序；严格的可逆性保证无损重建回原始特征空间；针对不同模型和时间步片段分别训练预测器，以适应异构特征动态。

Result: 在FLUX、Qwen-Image和HunyuanVideo上的实验表明，LinCa仅增加不到0.2%的额外参数，显著优于现有方法，并在5-7倍加速下保持近乎无损的质量。

Conclusion: LinCa验证了通过可逆网络分解特征并差异化预测是解决高加速比下特征缓存质量退化的有效方案，为扩散模型的高效部署提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in image and video generation, yet the high computational cost of iterative sampling remains a critical bottleneck for practical deployment. Feature caching has emerged as a promising acceleration paradigm by reusing or predicting intermediate features across timesteps. However, existing training-free methods apply uniform prediction strategies that cannot adapt to the heterogeneous feature dynamics, causing significant quality degradation under high acceleration ratios. We propose LinCa, a feature caching framework based on learnable invertible networks. LinCa decomposes cached features into sub-components with distinct continuity properties via a lightweight invertible network and applies differentiated prediction orders matched to each component. The strict invertibility guarantees lossless reconstruction back to the original feature space, forming a unified Decompose-Predict-Reconstruct pipeline. By training separate predictors for different models and timestep segments, LinCa adapts to heterogeneous feature dynamics. Experiments on FLUX, Qwen-Image, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate that LinCa, with less than 0.2% additional parameters, significantly outperforms existing methods and maintains near-lossless quality at 5-7x speedup. Code: https://github.com/QHR69/LinCa

</details>


### [80] [Dual Co-Train: Cross-Dataset Ultrasound Tongue Segmentation Under Extreme Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17983)
*Alisher Myrgyyassov,Zhen Song,Bruce Xiao Wang,Yu Sun,Min Ney Wong,Yihao Zhou,Yongping Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于源域免访问（source-free）域适应框架的超声舌头分割方法，以轻量级UltraUNet为骨干网络，通过迭代伪标签精炼、轮廓质量控制模块和分割引导的条件GAN生成目标风格合成数据，在12个源-目标跨域组合和8个数据集上优于包括监督方法在内的所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声舌头轮廓分割在跨数据集域迁移下仍具挑战性，有限的标注、探头差异和采集噪声会显著降低模型泛化能力。现有方法在源数据不可用或源模型欠拟合（仅5张标注图像预训练）的情况下难以有效适应目标域。

Method: 提出一个源免域适应框架：1）以仅5张源标注图像预训练的超轻量UltraUNet为起点，模拟欠拟合的受限源模型；2）在完全无标注的目标域上迭代精炼伪标签；3）利用基于轮廓的质量控制模块过滤不可靠分割掩膜；4）通过分割引导的条件GAN生成目标风格合成图像-掩膜对；5）学生模型在干净伪标签、带一致性正则化的噪声伪标签和合成样本的混合数据上训练，实现无需源数据的闭环适应。

Result: 在8个超声舌头成像数据集的12个源-目标迁移组合上，方法在分割重叠度和轮廓精度上均优于基线（包括监督方法）。此外还进行了源尺寸缩放实验和消融研究验证各组件有效性。

Conclusion: 任务特定的伪标签精炼和目标风格合成增强能够显著提升超声舌头成像的源免域适应性能，为受限源模型下的跨域分割提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Ultrasound tongue contour segmentation remains challenging under cross-dataset domain shift, where limited annotations, probe variability, and acquisition noise often degrade model generalization. We present a source-free domain adaptation framework for robust ultrasound tongue segmentation built on a lightweight UltraUNet backbone. Starting from a checkpoint pretrained on only five labeled source images, simulating an underfitted constrained source model, the proposed method adapts to a fully-unlabeled target domain by iteratively refining pseudo-labels, filtering unreliable masks with a contour-based quality-control module, and generating target-style synthetic image-mask pairs through a segmentation-guided conditional GAN. The student model is then trained on a mixture of clean pseudo-labeled target images, noisy pseudo-labels with consistency regularization, and synthetic samples, enabling closed-loop adaptation without access to source data. We evaluate the method on 12 source-target transfer pairs across eight ultrasound tongue imaging datasets, and conduct source-size scaling experiments and ablation studies. Across all comparisons, the proposed framework improves segmentation overlap and contour accuracy over the baselines, including supervised ones. These results suggest that task-specific pseudo-label refinement and synthetic target-style augmentation can substantially improve source-free adaptation for ultrasound tongue imaging.

</details>


### [81] [GS-Voxel: Fitting-Free Structured Latents for Large-Scale 3DGS Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17988)
*Ming Qian,Zijian Wang,Minchao Sun,Jincheng Xiong,Hang Zhang,Mu Xu,Chi Wang,Baoquan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GS-Voxel是一个无需拟合的结构化潜空间框架，可将预优化的3D高斯场景确定性转换为稀疏活跃体素，并通过GS专用因子化VAE编码为稀疏3D潜变量，支持大规模航拍3D高斯场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可扩展的潜3D生成器依赖结构化张量，而预优化的3DGS重建是无序、空间不规则且图元数量差异巨大的，二者之间存在表示鸿沟。

Method: 提出了GS-Voxel框架，包含三个核心组成：确定性地将兼容的预优化3DGS转换为稀疏活跃体素（无需每场景优化）；GS专用的因子化VAE分别编码体素几何与局部高斯属性为稀疏3D潜变量，其大小随占用体素数增长；在GS-Voxel潜空间中训练图像条件流模型以生成航拍3DGS场景。

Result: 实验表明GS-Voxel能为预优化航拍3DGS重建提供结构化潜变量，潜容量随占用体素数增长；同时支持重叠感知的分块推理，实现超出单训练裁剪范围的大面积场景生成。

Conclusion: GS-Voxel有效弥合了预优化3DGS与结构化潜生成器之间的鸿沟，为大规模航拍场景合成提供了一种拟合免费、容量可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Many scalable latent 3D generators operate on structured tensors, whereas pre-optimized 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstructions are unordered, spatially irregular, and vary widely in primitive count. We present GS-Voxel, a fitting-free structured latent framework, and evaluate it for large-scale aerial 3D Gaussian scene generation. GS-Voxel deterministically converts a compatible pre-optimized 3DGS reconstruction into sparse active voxels without additional per-scene optimization, retaining the sub-voxel positions and rendering attributes of the selected primitives. A GS-specific factorized VAE then separately encodes voxel geometry and local Gaussian attributes into sparse 3D latents whose size grows with the number of occupied voxels rather than being limited by a fixed scene-wide primitive count. We train image-conditioned flow models in the GS-Voxel latent space to generate aerial 3DGS scenes. A key application enabled by GS-Voxel is large-area scene generation: overlap-aware tiled inference extends synthesis beyond a single training crop conditioned on satellite-view images. Our results show that GS-Voxel provides structured latents for pre-optimized aerial 3DGS reconstructions, with latent capacity that grows with the number of occupied voxels.

</details>


### [82] [AViTS: Adaptive Spatiotemporal Token Selection for Efficient Dynamic-Resolution Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17995)
*Haoran Qin,Zhengan Yan,Shikang Zheng,Xiaobing Tu,Jiacheng Liu,Yuqi Lin,Chang Zou,JinShan Liu,Peiliang Cai,Xiantao Zhang,Jinkui Ren,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AViTS提出了一种自适应时空令牌选择框架，通过融合空间重要性（潜在文本注意力）和时间重要性（令牌特征随扩散步变化的动态）实现动态分辨率DiTs上的选择性上采样，在保持生成质量的同时大幅降低计算开销，最高可减少约9倍FLOPs，与蒸馏模型结合可达14.76倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiTs）生成质量高但迭代采样成本昂贵。现有动态分辨率采样在分辨率转换时统一上采样所有潜在令牌，导致冗余计算和细节一致性下降。已有的部分上采样策略多依赖局部潜在结构线索或单步统计，难以联合捕捉令牌-文本语义相关性和跨扩散步的令牌表示动态。

Method: AViTS通过潜在文本注意力建模空间重要性，通过令牌级特征在扩散时间步中的变化建模时间重要性，并将两者融合实现时空重要性感知的选择性上采样。优先对关键令牌进行分辨率细化，延迟非重要令牌的上采样，从而减少冗余高分辨率计算。

Result: 在FLUX上实现高达6.34倍加速，在Qwen-Image-Edit和FLUX.1-Kontext-dev上实现近9倍FLOPs缩减，与蒸馏、量化和特征缓存正交，结合蒸馏模型可达14.76倍。

Conclusion: AViTS有效改善了动态分辨率DiTs中质量-效率的权衡，且可与其他效率优化技术正交叠加，展示了在生成模型推理加速方面的良好前景。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) achieve high-quality generation but are costly due to iterative sampling. Dynamic-resolution sampling reduces early-stage cost by denoising at low resolution; however, uniformly upsampling all latent tokens at resolution transitions incurs redundant computation and may degrade fine-detail consistency. Existing partial upsampling strategies typically rely on local latent structure cues or single-step statistics, making it difficult to jointly capture token-text semantic relevance and token-wise representation dynamics across diffusion steps. We propose AViTS, an adaptive spatiotemporal token selection framework for dynamic-resolution DiTs. AViTS models spatial importance via latent-text attention and temporal importance via token-level feature variation across diffusion timesteps, and fuses them to enable spatiotemporal importance-aware selective upsampling: it prioritizes resolution refinement for critical tokens while deferring less important ones, thereby reducing redundant high-resolution computation and improving the quality-efficiency trade-off. AViTS achieves up to 6.34x on FLUX and nearly 9x FLOPs reduction on Qwen-Image-Edit and FLUX.1-Kontext-dev, orthogonal to distillation, quantization, and feature caching, and reaching 14.76x with distilled models. Code: https://github.com/QHR69/AViTS

</details>


### [83] [Memory Tree Guided Key Frame Querying for Efficient 3D Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18009)
*Hsiang-Wei Huang,Fu-Chen Chen,Li-Wu Tsao,Cheng-Han Lee,Che-Chun Su,Lu Xia,Ronghui Peng,Jenq-Neng Hwang,Min Sun,Cheng-Hao Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MemTree3D，一种记忆树引导的关键帧选择范式，用于具身场景中的高效3D问答。该方法利用紧凑可复用的3D场景表示，支持实时在线构建，通过评分机制让LLM高效检索与问题相关的关键帧，无需重新处理整个视频流。在OpenEQA上，将GPT-4o的LLM-Match提升17.4%，LLaVA-OneVision-7B提升5.8%，优于现有视觉搜索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法采用视觉搜索式的关键帧检索方式，需要针对每个用户查询在数千个视频帧中进行搜索，效率低下。具身场景中VLM推理受限于有限的计算和内存资源，需要更高效的关键帧选择方法。

Method: 提出记忆树引导的关键帧选择范式：构建名为MemTree3D的紧凑可复用3D场景表示，利用相机6-DoF位姿支持实时在线构建；MemTree3D捕获多层级3D场景信息，通过评分式帧选择机制使LLM能够高效查询和检索与问题相关的关键帧，无需重新处理整个视频流。

Result: 在OpenEQA基准上，该方法使GPT-4o的LLM-Match提升17.4%，LLaVA-OneVision-7B提升5.8%，超越了现有视觉搜索方法。

Conclusion: MemTree3D通过高效的记忆树结构替代低效的视觉搜索方法，实现了具身场景中更快速、更准确的3D问答，代码已开源。

Abstract: Answering questions accurately and efficiently in embodied scenarios presents significant challenges due to limited computational and memory resources for Vision Language Model (VLM) inference. Existing methods adopt visual search key frame retrieval method to select critical question-related key frames for VLM input. However, visual search methods are inefficient because they require visual search among thousands of video frames for each individual user query. In this work, we propose a memory tree guided key frame selection paradigm for efficient 3D question answering in embodied scenarios. Our method leverages a compact and reusable 3D scene representation, termed MemTree3D, which supports real-time online construction leveraging camera 6-DoF poses. MemTree3D captures multi-level 3D scene information, enabling a Large Language Model to efficiently query and retrieve question-relevant key frames through our scoring-based frame selection without reprocessing the entire video stream. On OpenEQA, our method improves the LLM-Match of GPT-4o by 17.4%, LLaVA-OneVision-7B by 5.8%, outperforms existing visual search methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hsiangwei0903/MemTree3D

</details>


### [84] [Automated ACL Footprint Identification Using 3D Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18012)
*Ruida Cheng,Ali Uneri,Gabriel Gibson,Frances T. Sheehan,Barry Boden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究开发了两种三维深度学习模型（基于3D股骨网格的图卷积网络模型和基于3D MR图像的标记增强识别模型），用于ACL股骨足迹中心的自动识别。在4883个右膝和3087个左膝图像集上测试，图像法优于模型法（平均误差2.1mm vs 2.8mm），为ACL重建术中隧道精准定位提供了可行的临床方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACL重建失败最常见的原因之一是股骨隧道位置不当，而精确保留ACL股骨足迹对术后膝关节力学恢复和预防移植物失败至关重要。现有AI研究主要聚焦于ACL分割和撕裂分类，利用深度学习方法识别ACL足迹中心的研究尚属空白，因此有必要探索3D深度模型在此任务上的可行性。

Method: 开发了两种3D深度学习架构：（1）基于3D股骨网格的3D图卷积神经网络几何模型；（2）基于3D MR图像的3D标记增强识别模型。使用公开数据库中4883个右膝和3087个左膝图像集，按80%/20%划分训练集和测试集进行模型生成与验证。

Result: 两种模型均表现出色，但基于图像的3D标记增强模型优于基于网格的几何模型，平均误差分别为2.1mm和2.8mm。

Conclusion: 3D深度学习为ACL足迹定位提供了可行的临床方法，有望提升ACL重建手术中足迹识别的精准度，从而改善手术预后并降低移植物失败风险。

Abstract: One of the most common reasons for anterior cruciate ligament (ACL) reconstruction failure is femoral tunnel malpositioning (ACL footprint center and tunnel orientation). Such failures may lead to the development of meniscal pathology and osteoarthritis. Accurate ACL femoral footprint identification is therefore essential for precise tunnel placement, restoration of the native knee joint mechanics, post-surgical knee joint health and prevention of graft failure. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) bring new opportunities to improve image-guided orthopedic surgery. However, at present, existing AI research focuses primarily on ACL segmentation and rupture classification based on pre- and post-operative magnetic resonance (MR) images. Identification of the ACL footprint center using deep learning methods has not been thoroughly researched. Thus, the purpose of this study is to explore 3D deep learning models for ACL femoral footprint identification directly from 3D MR images. Two comprehensive 3D deep learning architectures were developed: a 3D graph convolutional neural network-based geometric model applied to 3D femoral meshes; and a 3D landmark-enhanced identification model based on 3D MR images. A total of 4883 right and 3087 left knee image sets were used from a publicly available database. Eighty percent (80%) were applied to model generation, and twenty percent (20%) were preserved for model testing. Both models achieved excellent performance; however, the image-based method outperformed the model-based method (average error of 2.1mm vs 2.8 mm). Thus, 3D deep learning provides a feasible clinical approach for ACL footprint localization and has the potential to improve ACL reconstruction footprint accuracy.

</details>


### [85] [Initialization-Free Bundle Adjustment Revisited: A Controlled Experimental Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18028)
*Simon Weber,Mateo de Mayo,Je Hyeong Hong,Carl Olsson,Daniel Cremers,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对无初始化光束法平差（InitFree BA）进行了系统性的实验研究，构建了统一评估框架（C++实现与Blender数据集生成器），揭示了低OSE目标值与有效度量3D重建之间存在被忽视的"优化-重建差距"，并识别出初始化先验、地标观测密度和度量升级稳定性为关键影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有InitFree BA方法的评估主要关注优化是否成功（即OSE目标值是否降低），但未明确回答一个更根本的问题：低OSE目标是否意味着有效的度量3D重建。这一评估盲区可能导致对方法的误判，亟需系统的实验验证。

Method: 构建统一评估框架：(1) C++实现现有基于OSE和VarPro的优化方法；(2) 使用基于Blender的数据集生成器提供精确真值、可控相机配置与观测密度；(3) 设计实验对比低OSE解与其度量升级后的重建质量，分析关键影响因素。

Result: 发现存在显著的优化-重建差距：OSE值相近的投影解在度量升级后可产生差异巨大的欧氏重建；确定初始化先验、地标观测密度和度量升级稳定性是决定重建成功的关键因素。

Conclusion: InitFree BA的核心挑战并非单纯最小化OSE目标，而是获得能够可靠支持度量升级的投影重建。本文提供的基准、实现与分析为未来研究奠定了更坚实的实验基础。

Abstract: Initialization-free bundle adjustment (InitFree BA) aims to recover camera poses and scene structure directly from image observations, avoiding the geometric initialization stages of conventional structure-from-motion pipelines. Recent methods based on Object-Space Error (OSE) formulations and Variable Projection (VarPro) show encouraging optimization behavior from random camera configurations. However, existing evaluations primarily measure optimization success, leaving unclear whether a low OSE objective yields a valid metric 3D reconstruction. We revisit InitFree BA experimentally through a unified evaluation framework combining a C++ implementation of existing OSE formulations with a Blender-based dataset generator providing exact ground truth and controlled camera configurations and observation densities. Our experiments reveal a previously overlooked optimization--reconstruction gap: projective solutions with similarly low OSE values can lead to substantially different Euclidean reconstructions after metric upgrade. We identify initialization priors, landmark observation density, and metric-upgrade stability as key factors governing reconstruction success. Overall, our results suggest that the main challenge of InitFree BA is not merely minimizing OSE objectives, but obtaining projective reconstructions that admit reliable metric upgrade. We believe that the proposed benchmark, implementation, and analysis establish stronger experimental foundations for future research on initialization-free bundle adjustment, a problem largely unexplored within the computer vision community. Project page is available at https://github.com/simonwebertum/InitFreeBA.git.

</details>


### [86] [Deep Academic Survey: Stateful Agentic Closed-Loop Paradigm for Academic Survey Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18034)
*Zhikai Xu,Zhucun Xue,Teng Hu,Yabiao Wang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAS，一个面向学术综述生成的状态化智能体框架，通过分离可复用的论文分析与特定主题的稿件构建，并引入DAS-Bench基准与DAS-Eval评估体系，在多项指标上优于现有最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术综述在组织快速增长的文献中至关重要，但构建综述需要大量的论文分析、知识组织、细粒度引文支持和可靠的稿件组装。现有深度研究及自动综述生成系统仅覆盖部分流程，未能在共享且可修订的状态下协调论文理解、文献组织、基于证据的写作和稿件验证。

Method: DAS的核心思想是分离可复用的论文分析与特定主题的稿件构建。它基于DAS-2M元数据湖（含约两百万篇论文的综述导向表示），通过维护显式的文献状态、组织结构状态、写作状态和定稿状态，采用候选驱动的分类规划、论文到章节的反向路由、以及分层声明与引用规划进行构建。语义评审仅重新激活受影响的写作状态进行修复与再评估，形成带确定性验证的范围化闭环。

Result: DAS在30个主题的全部四个维度中平均得分最高，总分4.34，优于最强竞争者的4.03；在匹配的21个CS子集上保持相同排序。盲法专家评估中，DAS在30个主题中的27个优于Naive RAG，在21个共享CS主题中的19个优于AutoSurvey。

Conclusion: DAS通过状态化智能体框架有效协调了综述生成的多个关键环节，在学术综述生成质量上显著超越现有系统，验证了分离论文分析与主题构建思路的有效性。

Abstract: Academic surveys play a central role in organizing rapidly expanding scholarly literature, yet their construction requires extensive paper analysis, coherent knowledge organization, fine-grained citation support, and reliable manuscript assembly. Existing Deep Research and automated survey generation systems address parts of this process, but typically do not coordinate paper understanding, literature organization, evidence-grounded drafting, and manuscript validation through a shared, revisable state. We introduce DAS, a stateful agentic framework for generating publication-oriented academic surveys. Its key idea is to separate reusable paper analysis from topic-specific manuscript construction. DAS builds on DAS-2M, a dynamically updated metadata lake containing survey-oriented representations of approximately two million papers. Its agents maintain explicit literature, organization, writing, and finalization states through candidate-grounded taxonomy planning, reverse paper-to-section routing, and hierarchical claim and citation planning. Semantic review reactivates only the affected writing states for repair and reevaluation, forming a scoped closed loop with deterministic validation. We further introduce DAS-Bench, a 30-topic benchmark, together with DAS-Eval, which assesses scholarly citation quality, taxonomic synthesis, hierarchical discourse, and manuscript assembly reliability through 16 criteria. Among systems evaluated on all 30 topics, DAS achieves the highest average in all four dimensions, with an overall score of 4.34 compared with 4.03 for the strongest competitor, and the same ordering is preserved on the matched 21-topic CS subset. Blinded expert evaluation further prefers DAS to Naive RAG on 27 of 30 topics and to AutoSurvey on 19 of 21 shared CS topics. The project page is available at https://zhikaixu24.github.io/projects/DAS/.

</details>


### [87] [Plug-and-Play Traffic Element Awareness for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18035)
*Zongzheng Zhang,Jijun Wang,Saining Zhang,Shuo Wang,Yiru Wang,Hai Yang,Yang Chen,Yuwen Heng,Hao Sun,Anqing Jiang,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了交通元素感知对端到端自动驾驶的影响。作者通过在多个公开驾驶数据集上补充全面的交通元素标注，构建了统一的研究基础设施，并采用最小侵入性的即插即用设计将交通元素信号集成到现有驾驶系统中。实验覆盖感知-预测-规划、视觉-语言-动作模型、回归规划器、扩散策略和轨迹评分等多种现代范式，在nuScenes、NAVSIM-v1/v2和Bench2Drive等数据集上均验证了该集成的有效性，并在NAVSIM-v2上取得了新的最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端驾驶研究主要关注动态交通参与者（如车辆和行人），而交通灯、路标等交通元素的作用很大程度上被忽视。公共数据集缺乏结构化交通元素标注，加上现代驾驶系统在架构和训练范式上差异巨大，导致学术界缺少对这些元素影响的系统性量化研究。

Method: 作者构建了统一的研究基础设施：为多个公开驾驶数据集补充全面的交通元素标注；采用一种极简且普适的集成设计，以即插即用的方式将交通元素信号融入现有管线，几乎不需要修改架构；在五种现代驾驶范式（感知-预测-规划、VLA模型、回归规划器、扩散策略、轨迹评分框架）和四个数据集上进行系统评估。

Result: 在所有范式和数据集上，简单集成交通元素信号均能一致地提升驾驶性能，证明了交通元素感知为端到端驾驶系统提供了稳健且可泛化的信号。特别是在具有挑战性的NAVSIM-v2基准上，该方法显著提升了最先进的架构和数据管线，确立了新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 交通要素感知是端到端自动驾驶中一个被忽视但至关重要的信号来源；通过统一的基础设施和即插即用设计，交通元素感知能够为跨范式、跨数据集的驾驶系统带来一致的性能提升，为未来自动驾驶研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Traffic elements such as traffic lights and road signs play a fundamental role in human driving decisions and should naturally influence end-to-end driving performance. However, existing end-to-end driving research predominantly focuses on dynamic road participants (e.g., vehicles and pedestrians), while the role of traffic elements remains largely unexplored. The community still lacks a systematic study quantifying their impact, largely because public datasets rarely provide structured traffic-element annotations and modern driving systems vary widely in architecture and training paradigm. In this work, we present the first systematic investigation of traffic element awareness for end-to-end autonomous driving. We construct a unified research infrastructure by augmenting multiple public driving datasets with comprehensive traffic-element annotations. To support diverse model families, we adopt a minimal and universal integration design that incorporates traffic-element signals into existing pipelines in a plug-and-play manner with negligible architectural modification. We evaluate this design across modern paradigms, including perception-prediction-planning pipelines, vision-language-action models (VLA), regression-based planners, diffusion-based policies, and trajectory-scoring frameworks, on nuScenes, NAVSIM-v1, NAVSIM-v2, and Bench2Drive. Across all paradigms and datasets, this simple integration consistently improves driving performance, demonstrating that traffic element awareness provides a robust and generalizable signal for end-to-end driving systems. Notably, on the challenging NAVSIM-v2 benchmark, our approach significantly improves state-of-the-art architectures and data pipelines, establishing a new state of the art.

</details>


### [88] [EDITBRIDGE: Towards Faithful and Efficient Ultra-High-Resolution Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18063)
*Jiayi Song,Shijie Huang,Fangtai Wu,Yubo Huang,Zhenxiong Tan,Songhua Liu,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EditBridge，一种扩散桥框架，用于高效超高分辨率图像编辑。它通过将细化建模为从低分辨率编辑结果到高分辨率结果的结构化数据到数据翻译，并以原始高分辨率源为显式条件来保留真实细节。通过先验引导的块状稀疏注意力机制，实现了高达4K分辨率的编辑，速度提升3.6-8.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像编辑在实际工作流中需求日益增长，但现有基于扩散的模型因二次注意力复杂度和内存限制，只能处理1K以下分辨率。常见的两阶段管线（低分辨率编辑+独立超分辨率）存在信息发散（幻觉细节与原始高分辨率源矛盾）和纹理退化（过度平滑或过度锐化伪影）两大关键问题。

Method: EditBridge采用扩散桥框架，将编辑细化任务形式化为从低分辨率编辑结果到高分辨率结果的结构化数据到数据翻译，并以原始高分辨率源图像为显式条件来保留真实细节。为高效整合高分辨率源引导，设计了先验引导的块状稀疏注意力机制，利用第一阶段编辑产生的语义对应关系，将跨图像交互限制在空间对齐区域内，大幅降低计算开销。

Result: 大量实验表明，EditBridge在高达4K分辨率下实现了高保真编辑和优越的感知质量，在2K分辨率下获得3.6-8.4倍加速，并能在61秒内完成实际4K编辑。

Conclusion: EditBridge通过扩散桥框架和先验引导的稀疏注意力，有效解决了传统两阶段高分辨率编辑中的信息发散和纹理退化问题，实现了高效且高保真的超高分辨率图像编辑。

Abstract: High-resolution image editing is increasingly demanded in professional workflows, yet existing diffusion-based models remain constrained to resolutions below 1K due to quadratic attention complexity and prohibitive memory requirements. A prevalent workaround employs a two-stage pipeline: editing at low resolution followed by independent super-resolution. However, this approach suffers from two critical issues: information divergence, where hallucinated details contradict the original high-resolution (HR) source, and texture degradation, manifesting as over-smoothed or over-sharpened artifacts. We propose EditBridge, a diffusion bridge framework for efficient ultra high-resolution editing. Unlike conventional diffusion that regenerates from noise, we formulate refinement as structured data-to-data translation from the low-resolution (LR) edited result to its HR counterpart, explicitly conditioned on the original HR source to preserve authentic details. To efficiently incorporate HR source guidance, we introduce a prior-guided block-wise sparse attention mechanism that exploits semantic correspondence from first-stage editing to constrain cross-image interactions to spatially aligned regions, significantly reducing computational overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EditBridge achieves high-fidelity editing with superior perceptual quality at resolutions up to 4K, delivering 3.6--8.4$\times$ speedup at 2K and enabling practical 4K editing in 61 seconds.

</details>


### [89] [From Corpora to Co-Evolving Capabilities: Capability-Centric Data Design for Generalist Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18076)
*Xingjian Wang,Zhao Wang,Taihang Hu,Jun Zheng,Qing Jin,Qinye Zhou,Zhengtao Wu,Yongchao Du,Zuan Gao,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Xiaoli Xu,Zhengze Xu,Hao Yan,Yuhang Yu,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能力驱动的数据基础设施，通过构建能力特异的监督数据和能力对齐的课程调度，实现大规模图像生成模型的高效训练。该框架包含三个相互协作的数据引擎，分别负责文本-图像接地、跨图像变换和图像-知识关联的监督构建，并通过多阶段课程学习按能力获取顺序逐步演进任务组成、视觉概念分布、数据质量和图像分辨率。最终构建了440M图像的T2I语料、120M编辑对和2700万+图像-实体对，成功训练了3B和6B两种规模的多模态扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像生成数据流水线通常孤立地优化各自任务特定的数据集，缺乏对不同生成能力之间依赖关系的系统性考虑。核心挑战不仅在于如何整理每个任务专属语料，还在于如何根据生成能力之间的依赖关系来组织异构监督信号，以支持多能力的高效学习和迁移。

Method: 提出能力驱动的数据基础设施，包含：(1) 三个专化但可互操作的数据引擎，分别构建文本-图像接地、跨图像变换和图像-知识关联的互补关系监督；(2) 描述专家跨任务和粒度对齐T2I与编辑监督；(3) 多阶段课程框架按能力获取的依赖顺序联合演进任务组成、视觉概念分布、数据质量和图像分辨率；(4) 能力感知评估闭环，通过目标检索、专家构建和差距感知重采样迭代优化。

Result: 框架在规模化下构建了440M图像T2I语料、120M编辑对和超过2700万图像-实体对，基于该基础设施从零训练了3B和6B两种规模的多模态扩散模型。在CPI-Bench上进行了定量评估，并在多种T2I和编辑场景下做了定性评估，结果显示广泛的视觉覆盖、多样的渲染能力和跨生成能力的有效迁移。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，能力驱动的数据基础设施能有效支持大规模生成模型的多能力协同训练，实现跨生成能力的良好迁移，验证了按照能力依赖关系组织异构监督信号这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Large-scale image generation has benefited from advances in data scale, quality, rebalancing, and recaptioning, yet conventional pipelines typically optimize task-specific datasets in isolation. A central challenge is not only how to curate each task-specific corpus, but also how to organize heterogeneous supervision according to the dependencies among generative capabilities. We present a \textbf{capability-driven data infrastructure} that couples capability-specific supervision construction with capability-aligned curriculum scheduling. Its three specialized yet interoperable data engines build complementary relational supervision for text-image grounding, inter-image transformation, and image-knowledge association, while caption experts align T2I and editing supervision across tasks and granularities. A multi-stage curriculum jointly evolves task composition, visual-concept distribution, data quality, and image resolution along the dependency order of capability acquisition, with capability-aware evaluation closing the loop through targeted retrieval, expert construction, and gap-aware resampling. At scale, the framework curates a 440M-image T2I corpus, 120M editing pairs, and over 27M image-entity pairs. With this infrastructure, we train multimodal diffusion models at two scales from scratch, with 3B and 6B sizes respectively. We conduct quantitative evaluation on CPI-Bench, along with qualitative evaluations across diverse text-to-image and editing scenarios. Experimental results present broad visual coverage, versatile rendering, and effective transfer across generative capabilities.

</details>

### [90] [On computational approaches to Pop music culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17812)
*Arthur Flexer*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文论证了流行音乐文化的计算研究必须超越单纯的音频分析，采用多模态方式整合音频、视觉、文本与文化信息，并综述了已有的大规模定量研究，指出了多模态方法稀缺和语料库采样外部效度不足两大问题，最后提出了三个未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流行音乐文化是一个包含音频、视觉、文本和文化内涵的丰富综合体，现有的计算研究大多局限于音频分析，缺乏多模态的整合视角。作者认为需要借鉴数字人文中"远距离阅读"的思路，将算法扩展应用于专辑封面、音乐视频、歌词和音乐杂志等多元数据源。

Method: 本文为概述性综述文章，通过对已有"远距离阅读/聆听/观看"及多模态组合研究的回顾与批判性分析，识别领域中的开放性问题和研究空白，并在此基础上提出三个示范性的未来研究路径。

Result: 综述发现两个主要问题：一是真正多模态方法的研究严重稀缺；二是音乐语料库构建中的采样实践导致外部效度存疑。基于此，作者提出了歌词主题宇宙测绘、专辑封面图像志建构、音乐时间线风格回潮追踪三条研究路径。

Conclusion: 流行音乐文化的计算研究需要走向整合式的多模态范式，将流行音乐视为社会文化现象进行远距离分析。未来的研究应在构建更具外部效度的语料库基础上，系统性地推进多模态方法的开发与应用。

Abstract: This overview article presents arguments why the computational study of Pop music culture needs to be conducted in a multi-modal way beyond mere audio analysis, gives a survey of already published quantitative work on analyzing Pop music at scale, and discusses challenges and promising research avenues for future work.
  We argue that Pop music culture is a rich tapestry of audio, visual, textual and cultural connotations and relations which needs to be studied in an integrative way as a multi-modal socio-cultural phenomenon. What is needed is an approach which is reminiscent of "distant reading", i.e. algorithmic analysis of thousands of books as a research tool in digital humanities. In addition to listening to audio, algorithms need to view album artwork and music videos, to read meta-information, lyrics, music magazines and books.
  Our review of already available work on distant reading/listening/viewing and multi-modal combinations thereof reveals two major open issues: a scarcity of truly multi-modal approaches and questionable external validity rooted in sampling practices when building music corpora. In trying to overcome these shortcomings we sketch three exemplary avenues for future research on Pop music culture: charting the topic universe of music lyrics, providing an iconography of album cover art, tracking retro cycles in music's timeline.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [Proactive Road Safety Intervention in Australia: Predicting Risky Driving Hotspots from Connected Vehicle Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16913)
*Adriana-Simona Mihăiţă,Clarence Cheung,Artur Grigorev,Tuo Mao,David Lillo-Trynes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用澳大利亚大悉尼地区的网联车辆遥测数据，通过g力阈值（急刹车、急转弯、急加速）量化危险驾驶行为，在地方政府区域（LGA）层面检测和预测近距离事故风险驾驶事件。研究对比了八种预测模型，发现ARIMA（MAE:162.21）表现最佳，与传统集成学习和深度学习模型相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的道路安全监测是反应式的，依赖事故发生后的碰撞记录分析。在事故发生前主动识别高风险区域和危险驾驶行为是一个关键但尚未充分探索的挑战，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用悉尼Greater Sydney地区的网联车辆遥测数据，通过g力阈值（急刹车>0.6g、急转弯>0.47g、急加速>0.5g）量化危险驾驶行为，构建时空热力图识别高风险区域，并基准对比了八种预测模型，涵盖集成学习（随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM）、深度学习（LSTM、N-BEATS）和经典时间序列方法（ARIMA、指数平滑、Prophet）三大类。

Result: ARIMA取得最低平均绝对误差（MAE:162.21），与LSTM（MAE:163.92）表现相当，优于所有集成学习方法，N-BEATS的MAE为180.75。悉尼内城区和西部LGA（CBD、Parramatta、Bankstown）被识别为持续高风险区域。

Conclusion: 在训练数据量有限的情况下，简洁的时间序列模型与深度学习方法具有竞争力。基于物联网的网联车辆数据有望支持主动式道路安全干预，研究识别出的高风险区域值得采取有针对性的政策行动。

Abstract: Road safety monitoring has historically been reactive, relying on crash-record analysis after fatalities and injuries have already occurred. Proactive identification of high-risk locations and dangerous driving behaviour before incidents occur is a critical but underexplored challenge. This paper addresses this gap using connected vehicle telemetry data from Greater Sydney, Australia, to detect and forecast near-miss risky driving events at the Local Government Area (LGA) level. Risky driving is quantified through g-force thresholds (hard braking >0.6g, harsh cornering >0.47g, harsh acceleration >0.5g), and spatio-temporal heatmaps are constructed to identify high-risk zones. Eight predictive models are benchmarked across three families: ensemble learning (Random Forests, XGBoost, LightGBM), deep learning (LSTM, N-BEATS), and classical time-series methods (ARIMA, Exponential Smoothing, Prophet). ARIMA achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE: 162.21), performing comparably to LSTM (MAE: 163.92) and outperforming all ensemble methods, with N-BEATS reaching an MAE of 180.75. These results demonstrate that parsimonious time-series models are competitive with deep learning approaches when training data volume is limited. The study highlights the potential of IoT-based connected vehicle data to support proactive road safety interventions, with Sydney's inner and western LGAs (CBD, Parramatta, Bankstown) identified as persistent high-risk zones warranting targeted policy action.

</details>


### [92] [Detecting and Discriminating Operator Misspecification in Hybrid PDE-Parameter Learning: a Reference-Free Instrument, with Discrimination Bounded In Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16925)
*Eric Fock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种工具，能从单次拟合（无需oracle）中同时区分两类失效：混合PDE参数估计器设定的算子错误与参数不可识别问题。在自伴抛物型反问题上，信息矩阵统计量在正确设定下中位数为0.19，误拒率0.033（低于预设的0.10上限），在两类误设定下分别升至224和85并在所有重复中触发。在正确设定但不可识别的设计中，该统计量保持沉默（n=200时为0.050），而秩统计量在预设边界处坍缩至零，从而实现两类失效的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的精度检查（如域内RMSE）对误设定估计器是盲目的：误设定估计器的域内RMSE为2.7×10⁻²，低于观测噪声（σ≥0.05时），而系数在零噪声和最大噪声下分别错误29.7%和31.2%。因此需要一种能从单次拟合中区分算子错误与参数不可识别的工具。

Method: 构建基于信息矩阵的统计量，配以plug-in尺度和按种子参数估计。同时使用秩统计量在不同设计上捕捉不同失效模式。通过三种实验设计验证：正确设定、正确设定但不可识别、两类误设定。对比多种估计器架构（单参数曲线拟合、裸参数、49和241参数的多层感知机、物理信息网络）。

Result: 正确设定下信息矩阵统计量中位数0.19，误拒率0.033（低于0.10预注册上限）；两类误设定下统计量分别升至224和85并全数触发。不可识别设计下统计量保持安静（0.050，95%CI [0.024, 0.090]），秩统计量在预设边界c₅*=2.15×10⁻³处坍缩。所有标准估计器收敛到同一伪真实值（闭式匹配0.07%），而物理信息网络因复合目标收敛到不相交的另一个解。

Conclusion: 该工具的核心贡献是实现了对两类失效的分离（检测本身已有大量研究）。在样本内其表现为一个界，在样本外则提供方向。作者同时报告了工具的盲区、一个预注册的负结果（神经估计器在恢复任务上输给Tikhonov正则化反演），以及保证成立但训练网络违背该保证的假设条件。

Abstract: We build an instrument that reads, from a single fit and with no oracle, whether the operator a hybrid PDE-parameter estimator postulates is wrong-and separates that from a merely unidentifiable parameter. On one self-adjoint parabolic inverse problem, an information-matrix statistic with plug-in scale and per-seed parameter has median 0.19 under correct specification, rejection rate $0.033$ against a pre-registered ceiling of $0.10$, and rises to $224$ and $85$ under two misspecifications, firing in every replicate. On a correctly specified but non-identifiable design it stays mute-$0.050$ at $n=200$, Clopper-Pearson $[0.024, 0.090]$-while a rank statistic collapses to zero at a pre-registered boundary $c_5^*=2.15\times10^{-3}.$ Two readings of one fit therefore separate the two failures across the three designs a deployable test reaches. That separation is the contribution; detection alone is a crowded flank. In sample it is a bound, out of sample a direction. It is needed because the usual accuracy check is blind: the misspecified estimator's in-domain RMSE is $2.7\times 10^{-2}$, below the observation noise for $σ\geq 0.05,$ while the coefficient is wrong by $29.7\%$ at zero noise, $31.2\%$ at the loudest. Nor is the failure architectural: a one-parameter curve fit, a bare parameter and multilayer perceptrons of $49$ and $241$ parameters converge to the same pseudo-true, matched in closed form to $0.07\%,$ whereas a physics-informed network, with its composite objective, converges to a disjoint one. We report where the instrument is blind, a pre-registered negative where a neural estimator loses to Tikhonov-regularized inversion at recovery, and the hypothesis under which its guarantee holds but a trained network violates it.

</details>


### [93] [Data-DPO: Direct Preference Optimization for Target Model Data Selection in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16926)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Data-DPO，一种面向目标模型的SFT数据选择方法，通过单步探针观察目标模型的本地训练反馈，将样本间激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量级奖励模型学习目标模型感知的数据偏好，最终结合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边际多样性构建稳定的训练子集，在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法通常将数据价值视为相对静态的属性，忽视了数据与目标模型能力分布之间的兼容性，导致选择的数据集难以充分发挥目标模型的实际训练效果。

Method: Data-DPO通过三步实现数据选择：(1)通过单步探针观察目标模型在不同样本上的本地训练反馈；(2)将样本间的激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量级奖励模型来学习目标模型感知的数据偏好；(3)在最终选择阶段，结合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边际多样性构建更稳定有效的训练子集。

Result: 在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT数据集上的实验表明，Data-DPO在多种数据预算下一致优于现有数据选择基线，且稳定超越使用全部数据的训练性能。

Conclusion: Data-DPO通过将数据选择从静态评估转变为目标模型感知的动态偏好学习，有效解决了数据与模型能力分布不匹配的问题，为SFT数据选择提供了新思路。

Abstract: Data selection in supervised fine-tuning aims to select a small set of effective samples from large-scale candidate data, reducing training cost while preserving model performance. However, existing methods usually treat data value as a relatively static property, and pay limited attention to the compatibility between data and the capability distribution of the target model. To address this issue, we propose Data-DPO, a target model-oriented SFT data selection method. Data-DPO observes the local training feedback of the target model on different samples through one-step probing, transforms activation differences among samples into pairwise data preferences, and trains a lightweight reward model to learn target-model-aware data preferences. In the final selection stage, Data-DPO further combines target model preference, external quality scores, and marginal diversity to construct a more stable and effective training subset. Experimental results on Vision-Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that Data-DPO consistently outperforms existing data selection baselines under multiple data budgets and stably surpasses full data training performance.

</details>


### [94] [Hierarchical Data Selection via Manifold Coverage and Sparse Feature Coverage in LLM Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16927)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MASS通过粗到细的分层覆盖策略进行数据选择，先用密集自编码器学习低维主流形坐标进行粗粒度语义分组，再用TopK稀疏自编码器在每个组内进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖，在Vision Flan和LLaVA-CoT上超过强基线，部分设置下仅用小量子集即可匹敌甚至超越全量训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着监督微调数据规模扩大，从大规模候选池中选择高价值子集对降低训练成本和提升模型性能至关重要。现有方法直接在原始嵌入空间中衡量多样性，几何度量会混淆主导语义方向、细粒度监督差异和局部噪声，限制了数据选择的效果。

Method: MASS将数据选择建模为粗到细的分层覆盖问题：(1) 使用密集自编码器学习低维主流形坐标，实现粗粒度语义分组；(2) 在每个语义组内，使用TopK稀疏自编码器进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖，从而在解耦主导语义方向的同时捕捉细粒度差异。

Result: 在Vision Flan和LLaVA-CoT数据集上的实验表明，MASS在各种数据预算下一致地优于强数据选择基线方法，并在多个设定中以较小的数据子集匹配或超越了全量数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: MASS通过分层解耦原始嵌入空间中的不同信息维度，实现了更高效、更高质量的数据选择，证明了将数据选择从单一空间度量转变为分层覆盖问题的有效性。

Abstract: As supervised fine-tuning data continues to scale, selecting high-value subsets from large candidate pools is crucial for reducing training cost and improving model performance. Existing methods often measure diversity directly in the original embedding space, where geometric metrics entangle dominant semantic directions, fine-grained supervision differences, and local noise. We address this limitation by formulating data selection as a coarse-to-fine hierarchical coverage problem and propose MASS. MASS learns low-dimensional principal manifold coordinates with a dense autoencoder for coarse semantic grouping, and then performs quality-aware sparse feature coverage within each group using a TopK sparse autoencoder. Experiments on Vision Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that MASS consistently outperforms strong data selection baselines across multiple budgets, and in several settings matches or surpasses full data training with only a small subset of data.

</details>


### [95] [Benchmarking Classical and Transformer-Based Models for Document Sensitivity Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16928)
*Aleesha Zainab,Muhammad Ahmed Khalid,Faheem Ullah Khan,Asifullah Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对组织文档敏感度分类中普遍存在的"标签泄漏"问题，构建了严格去泄漏控制的16,000份外交电文语料库（Strategic 16K），并对六种模型架构进行系统基准测试。实验表明BERT性能最优（准确率89.14%，F1=89.33%），TF-IDF+逻辑回归在经典模型中表现最佳且计算成本显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的文档敏感度分类方法虽具可扩展性，但训练数据中普遍存在标签泄漏问题——文档体内的残留分类标记使模型利用表面捷径而非学习真实的内容语义特征，导致性能评估虚高且不可靠，严重影响分类结果的可信度。

Method: 构建Strategic 16K语料库（16,000份来自WikiLeaks PlusD的外交电文），设计了扩展的去泄漏协议，识别并消除文档体内三类残留分类标记；随后对六种模型架构（涵盖经典机器学习和基于Transformer的方法）进行系统性基准测试。

Result: BERT在干净基准上表现最优（准确率89.14%，F1=89.33%），ELECTRA次之（准确率88.57%，F1=88.90%）；经典模型中TF-IDF+逻辑回归以显著更低的计算成本达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了首个在明确泄漏控制条件下基于WikiLeaks PlusD构建的完全可复现的敏感度分类基准，为组织文档敏感度分类领域建立了可靠评估的基础。

Abstract: Automatic sensitivity classification of organizational documents is a critical yet underserved problem, where the consequences of misclassification range from regulatory violations to security breaches. While AI-based approaches offer a scalable alternative to manual review, their reliability depends fundamentally on the integrity of training data. A pervasive but underreported problem in this domain is label leakage: residual classification markers embedded within document bodies that allow models to exploit surface shortcuts rather than learning genuine content-based sensitivity signals, producing performance estimates that are inflated and unreliable. This paper addresses this problem by introducing Strategic 16K, a carefully constructed, leakage-controlled corpus of 16,000 diplomatic cables sourced from the WikiLeaks Public Library of US Diplomacy (PlusD), and presents a systematic benchmark evaluating six model architectures spanning classical machine learning and transformer-based approaches. We document an extended leakage removal protocol that identifies and eliminates three categories of residual classification markers embedded within document bodies. On the clean benchmark, BERT achieves the strongest performance (Accuracy = 89.14%, F1 = 89.33%), followed by ELECTRA (Accuracy = 88.57%, F1 = 88.90%). Among classical models, TF-IDF with Logistic Regression achieves the strongest performance at significantly lower computational cost. These results constitute the first fully reproducible sensitivity classification benchmark constructed under explicit leakage-controlled conditions from WikiLeaks PlusD.

</details>


### [96] [Mr.Dec: Daily-Scale Longitudinal Multimodal Modeling for 30-Day Readmission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16929)
*Minjun Kim,Jong Hak Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Mr.Dec模型，通过Transformer解码器将住院期间每日多模态临床事件（EHR更新和间断性胸部X光片）按时间对齐建模，用于预测30天再入院风险，在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上取得SOTA性能，并能识别入院中的"关键日"提供可解释的风险分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将复杂的纵向住院历史压缩为固定表示，丢失了日级颗粒度的临床信号，无法捕捉患者在住院期间不断演变的生理状态和临床风险轨迹。

Method: Mr.Dec将每次入院建模为自然时间顺序的每日多模态事件序列，利用Transformer解码器整合每日EHR更新和间断性CXR结果（时间对齐流），并采用疾病特异性监督对比学习作为辅助正则化，在潜在空间中引导诊断感知结构。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上的评估表明，Mr.Dec通过保持临床序列的完整性取得了最先进的性能，并能识别入院内的"关键日"，为实时风险分层提供可操作的临床解释。

Conclusion: Mr.Dec通过保留每日多模态临床序列的完整性，有效解决了传统方法丢失日级信号的问题，不仅提升了30天再入院预测的准确性，还提供了具有临床意义的可解释性输出（关键日识别），有助于实时风险分层和医疗资源优化。

Abstract: Predicting 30-day hospital readmission is essential for assessing patient stability and optimizing healthcare resources. As clinical risk evolves with the accumulation of evidence during hospitalization, capturing these dynamic trajectories is essential. However, many existing approaches compress the complex longitudinal history into fixed representations, often losing the granular, day-level clinical signals that reflect a patient's evolving physiological state. To address this, we propose Mr.Dec (Multimodal Readmission-risk prediction Decoder), which models each admission as a natural chronological sequence of daily multimodal events. By leveraging a Transformer Decoder, Mr.Dec integrates daily Electronic Health Record(EHR) updates and intermittent Chest X-ray(CXR) findings in a time-aligned stream, reflecting the actual clinical workflow. To ensure robustness, we utilize Disease-Specific Supervised Contrastive Learning as an auxiliary regularization to induce a diagnosis-aware structure in the latent space. Evaluations on the MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-CXR datasets show that Mr.Dec achieves state-of-the-art performance by preserving the integrity of the clinical sequence. Furthermore, our model identifies "Critical Days" within an admission, providing actionable and clinically grounded interpretations for real-time risk stratification. Code is available at: https://github.com/yejix-ai/MR.DEC

</details>


### [97] [EMAN: Optimization-Driven Capacity Growth through Path Emergence in Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16930)
*Chenlei Fang,Jingchen Li,Hongzong LI,Qingyao Li,Yixuan Zhang,Huarui Wu,Haobin Shi,Chunjiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMAN框架，通过优化驱动的潜在相对相位检测，使网络能从单路径计算自适应生长出独立路径，仅在持续优化证据出现时才进行结构决策，在PASCAL-Context和NYUv2等数据集上以竞争性计算成本取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多任务学习方法的能力变化受限于预定义结构或由任务边界/冲突信号触发，缺乏真正数据驱动的动态结构扩展能力。

Method: 提出一个两阶段方案：(1) 通过潜在相对相位揭示反对称生长方向，无需实例化第二条路径；(2) 在训练过程中监控多个决策信号，将局部优化证据转化为结构决策，仅在认证后才物化两条等容量独立路径。

Result: 在受控秩设置、PASCAL-Context和NYUv2上的大量实验验证了有效性，在竞争性计算成本下实现了改进的性能。

Conclusion: EMAN实现了一种优化驱动的动态网络扩展机制，能够自适应分配共享和任务专属表示容量，为多任务学习提供了更灵活的结构进化路径。

Abstract: Existing multi-task learning methods rely on hard sharing, multiple paths or experts, adaptive sharing, and dynamic expansion. However, their capacity changes are usually constrained by predefined structures or triggered by task boundaries and conflict signals. This raises a fundamental question: can a network start from exact single-path computation and grow a new independent path only when persistent optimization evidence appears? We propose the Emergent Modular Atomic Network (EMAN), an optimization-driven framework for exposing an antisymmetric growth direction through latent relative phases without instantiating a second path, and for monitoring multiple decision signals during training to transform local optimization evidence into a structural decision. EMAN materializes two equal-capacity independent paths only after certification. EMAN adaptively allocates shared and task-specific representation capacity to accommodate varying task requirements. Extensive experiments on controlled rank settings, PASCAL-Context, and NYUv2 validate its effectiveness, achieving improved performance at a competitive computational cost.

</details>


### [98] [SW-ProxyCE: Zero-Query Adversarial Transfer from Public EEG Encoders to Private Downstream Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16931)
*Linhua Cong,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了EEG基础模型在"公共编码器-私有下游模型"部署场景下的对抗迁移攻击问题，提出Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE)攻击框架，通过收缩白化类原型恢复任务决策几何，实现无需查询和替代分类器的可迁移对抗样本生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型编码器的公开释放虽然促进了下游开发，但也引入了新的安全风险：公开可用的表示可能使私有下游模型暴露于对抗攻击之下。现有研究尚未探索这一安全漏洞。

Method: 提出SW-ProxyCE框架，攻击者对公开编码器拥有白盒访问权，仅使用小规模任务匹配的标记参考集，通过收缩白化的类原型恢复任务级决策几何，无需训练额外替代分类器即可生成可迁移的对抗样本。

Result: 在三个EEG任务、三种通用基础编码器和一个范式特定预训练编码器上评估，覆盖线性探针和全微调下游模型、跨被试和被试内场景。SW-ProxyCE生成的对抗样本能有效迁移到不可访问的下游模型，且一致优于任务无关的表示偏移攻击。

Conclusion: EEG基础模型的强迁移性并不意味着对抗鲁棒性，公开编码器部署存在实际安全威胁；SW-ProxyCE揭示了这一安全漏洞，代码将开源。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for EEG decoding by learning reusable representations from large-scale heterogeneous neural recordings. However, the open release of EEG foundation encoders, while facilitating downstream developments, also introduces a previously unexplored security risk: publicly available representations may make private downstream models vulnerable. This paper investigates adversarial transfer attacks in EEG foundation model deployment in a public-encoder and private-downstream setting, where attackers have white-box access to a released encoder and a small task-matched labeled reference set, but no access or query to victim parameters, outputs, or gradients. We propose Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE), a query-free task-aware attack framework that recovers task-level decision geometry from a small labeled reference set through shrinkage-whitened class prototypes, enabling transferable adversarial generation without training an additional surrogate classifier. We evaluated SW-ProxyCE across three EEG tasks using three general-purpose foundation encoders and a paradigm-specific pre-trained encoder, covering both linear-probing and full-fine-tuning downstream models in cross-subject and within-subject scenarios. Results demonstrated that adversarial examples generated from the public encoder and limited labeled references can effectively transfer to inaccessible downstream models. SW-ProxyCE consistently outperformed task-agnostic representation-shift attacks, revealing that the strong transferability of EEG foundation models does not necessarily lead to adversarial robustness. Our code will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [99] [DOW-KE: Anchor-Free Multi-Layer Knowledge Editing via Direct End-to-End Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16932)
*Ran Chen,Junbo Zhang,Qianli Zhou,Xinyang Deng,Wen Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DOW-KE是一种无锚点的知识编辑方法，核心原则是"优化的内容必须与部署的内容完全一致"，通过将最终编辑目标经完整模型反向传播，联合优化所有编辑层的权重更新，消除了锚点方法中传播衰减与失真的闭包差距。在两类数据集和三个模型的大规模顺序编辑中，六个模型-数据集设置里有五个取得了最高综合Score和邻域特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多层"定位-再编辑"方法存在闭包差距：优化的是中间表示（锚点激活），但部署的是多层权重更新，二者通过真实前向传播的联合效果从未被优化。每层更新来自局部求解，传播引起的衰减和失真无法校正。

Method: DOW-KE放弃锚点机制，将最终编辑目标通过完整模型反向传播，联合优化所有编辑层的权重更新，使跨层传播与耦合进入每个梯度步；同时将保护投影嵌入更新参数化的计算图中，使每个梯度作用于实际部署的更新，保证编辑不触及受保护知识。

Result: 在两类数据集和三个模型上执行大规模顺序编辑，DOW-KE在六个模型-数据集设置中的五个取得了最高的整体Score和邻域Specificity，优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: "优化即部署"原则是知识编辑中消除闭包差距的关键；将保护机制内置于计算图而非事后约束，能在保持编辑精度的同时有效保护邻域知识，为大规模顺序知识编辑提供了一种更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Multi-layer locate-then-edit methods for knowledge editing first optimize target residual-stream activations (anchors) at selected layers, then realize them layer by layer as weight updates. This pipeline optimizes an intermediate representation but deploys multi-layer weight updates whose joint effect through the true forward pass is never itself optimized: regardless of how anchors are set or propagated, each update comes from a local solve, so propagation-induced attenuation and distortion go uncorrected, leaving a closure gap between anchor targets and realized edits. We propose DOW-KE, an anchor-free method built on a single principle: what is optimized must be exactly what is deployed. DOW-KE backpropagates the final editing objective through the complete model, jointly optimizing the updates of all edited layers so cross-layer propagation and coupling enter every gradient step. The same principle dictates where preservation resides: embedding the preservation projection in the update parameterization, inside the computation graph, makes every gradient act on the deployed update; post-hoc constraints would reopen the gap, and the constrained search keeps edits clear of protected knowledge. In large-scale sequential editing on two datasets and three models, DOW-KE achieves the highest overall Score and neighborhood Specificity in five of six model-dataset settings among the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [100] [Study-Strategy Clusters from EdNet Logs Track Engagement, Not Mastery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16963)
*Qingchuan Lyu,Yingxin Li,Albert Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对EdNet-KT3数据集中5000名活跃学习者的智能辅导系统日志进行聚类分析，发现行为聚类虽能形成8种稳定的学习策略并预测后期参与度，但无法预测学习效果（未辅助准确率），且与知识追踪模型的学习掌握信号几乎无关（ARI=0.007），表明行为聚类描述的是学习风格和参与度而非知识获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习分析领域常将智能辅导系统日志的无监督聚类结果视为"学习者类型"并假设其能预测学习效果，作者旨在检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 对EdNet-KT3中5000名活跃学习者的学习策略特征（资源使用、复习、视频、题目练习）进行聚类分析，通过轮廓系数选取k=5的父级聚类，并对其残差（约64.9%）再次聚类得到4种更细风格，形成bootstrap稳定的8种命名策略层级。按响应次数将时间线分为前后两半，前一半用于聚类、后一半用于评估结果。同时训练SAKT知识追踪模型与仅知各节难度基线的对比实验，并用ARI度量聚类标签与掌握信号的关联。

Result: 早前期聚类能预测后期参与度（持续性η²≈0.106，完成度η²≈0.021），但不能预测后期未辅助准确率（校正p≈0.093）。SAKT模型在TOEIC七个考试部分的预测仅比基线提升0.051的AUC（CI [0.045,0.058]），且掌握信号与行为风格几乎独立（ARI=0.007）。仅用学习量聚类的标签与策略标签匹配度极低（ARI=0.064）。

Conclusion: 行为聚类能够刻画学习风格和参与度特征，但对知识增长的预测能力不足，挑战了"聚类即学习者类型可预测学习"的常见假设，提示学习分析应谨慎解读行为聚类结果。

Abstract: Learning analytics often treats unsupervised clusters of intelligent tutoring system (ITS) logs as learner types that should predict learning. We test that assumption on EdNet-KT3. Clustering study-strategy features (resource use, revision, video, problem practice) for 5{,}000 active learners yields a silhouette-selected parent cut ($k=5$) with 4 contrast poles (reading-focused, video-heavy, revision-heavy, and problem-first) plus a large near-mean residual ($\sim$64.9\%). Reclustering that residual adds four finer styles, giving a bootstrap-stable hierarchy of 8 named strategies. We split each learner's timeline by respond count so clusters use only the early half and outcomes only the late half. Early clusters predict later engagement (continuing to practice and finishing late sessions, especially persistence, $η^{2}\approx 0.106$; completion $η^{2}\approx 0.021$) but not later unassisted accuracy (correctness on late first-attempts without help; $p_{\mathrm{adj}}\approx 0.093$). Volume rises with some styles, yet volume-only clustering barely matches strategy labels (ARI$=0.064$). A knowledge-tracing model (SAKT) on the seven TOEIC exam sections predicts next correctness only modestly better than a baseline that knows only how hard each section usually is (AUC lift $+0.051$; CI $[+0.045,+0.058]$), and that mastery signal is nearly independent of behavior styles (ARI$=0.007$). Behavioral clustering here describes study styles and engagement, not knowledge gains.

</details>


### [101] [RoBell-RVFL: A Robust Generalized Bell Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16965)
*A. Rahaman,A. Quadir,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RoBell-RVFL，一种鲁棒且轻量级的质量感知广义贝尔随机向量功能链接网络，通过双策略样本级加权机制（少数类单位权重+多数类概率加权广义贝尔隶属函数）在核诱导特征空间中抑制噪声、边界和离群样本，在不牺牲RVFL闭式学习效率的前提下处理类别不平衡和标签噪声问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集中多数类占主导地位会使随机神经网络决策边界产生偏倚，忽略关键少数类样本。现有方法（如SMOTE和类加权损失函数）仅处理类别比例而忽视类内分布，对标签噪声和离群点脆弱，需要一种能同时应对类别不平衡和噪声数据的鲁棒方法。

Method: RoBell-RVFL采用双策略样本级加权机制：少数类样本使用单位权重严格保留其信息，多数类样本通过概率加权广义贝尔（gbell）隶属函数在核诱导特征空间中自适应调节其影响力。该方法显式整合局部类别概率和类别分布信息，实现样本贡献的自适应控制，同时保持RVFL网络的闭式学习效率。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上的广泛评估以及高达40%标签噪声的鲁棒性测试表明，RoBell-RVFL一致且显著优于最新的RVFL变体方法。

Conclusion: 自适应、质量感知的样本加权对鲁棒RVFL学习至关重要，传统的全局加权方案在噪声和不平衡环境中效果不佳，RoBell-RVFL有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: The dominance of majority classes in real-world datasets poses a fundamental challenge to randomized neural networks, often biasing decision boundaries and overlooking critical minority samples. Existing remedies, such as synthetic minority over-sampling (SMOTE) and class-weighted loss functions, primarily address class proportions while neglecting intra-class distribution, making them vulnerable to label noise and outliers. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RoBell-RVFL}, a robust and lightweight \emph{quality-aware} generalized bell random vector functional link network that redefines how randomized models handle class imbalance and noisy data. RoBell-RVFL employs a dual-strategy, sample-level weighting mechanism that strictly preserves minority class information using unit weights, while adaptively regulating the influence of majority class samples through a probability-weighted generalized bell (gbell) membership function in a kernel-induced feature space. This design effectively suppresses noisy, boundary, and outlier samples within the majority class, enabling the network to learn from informative samples rather than merely abundant ones. By explicitly incorporating local class probability and class distribution information into the learning process, RoBell-RVFL achieves adaptive control over sample contributions without sacrificing the closed-form learning efficiency of RVFL networks. Extensive evaluations on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, along with robustness tests under up to 40\% label noise, demonstrate that RoBell-RVFL consistently and significantly outperforms recent state-of-the-art RVFL variants. The results indicate that adaptive, quality-aware sample weighting is essential for robust RVFL learning, rendering conventional global weighting schemes ineffective in noisy and imbalanced environments.

</details>


### [102] [MultiSigBERT: Beyond Survival Analysis through Multimodal and Sequential Modeling in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16972)
*Paul Minchella,Stéphane Chrétien,Guillaume Metzler,Loïc Verlingue,Rémi Vaucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiSigBERT，一种基于路径签名表示的多模态时序生存分析框架，融合电子病历中的自由文本报告和结构化变量，通过BERT嵌入、PCA压缩、签名变换和LASSO-Cox模型实现肿瘤患者生存预测，在包含2500多名患者和12万份医疗报告的莱昂·贝朗中心真实队列上取得0.743的一致性指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生存模型大多局限于单一数据模态，未能利用电子病历中互补的多模态信息（叙述性文本、数值测量、结构化变量），也未能充分捕捉患者轨迹的时序特征，作者希望构建一个统一的框架来联合建模多模态时序数据以改善生存预测。

Method: MultiSigBERT框架包含四个关键步骤：1）利用BERT提取叙述性医疗报告的词嵌入并平均得到句子嵌入；2）通过模态特定的PCA对嵌入进行降维压缩；3）将压缩后的表示与结构化协变量拼接形成联合时序轨迹，并使用Rough Paths理论中的签名变换（Signature transform）高效编码跨模态的高阶时序交互（无需监督）；4）将签名特征输入LASSO正则化的Cox比例风险模型估计个体化风险评分。

Result: 在莱昂·贝朗中心的真实肿瘤学队列（包括超过2500名患者、12万份医疗报告及结构化记录）上进行评估，模型在独立测试集上取得0.743的一致性指数（标准差0.029），验证了联合建模多模态时序动态及患者层面几何结构对生存预测的显著优势。

Conclusion: MultiSigBERT证明了融合文本和非文本多模态数据、结合路径签名以捕获高阶时序交互，可以显著提升肿瘤学中的生存预测性能，为临床决策支持提供了从异构EHR数据中进行有效生存建模的新方案。

Abstract: Machine learning has become an essential component of modern healthcare, where the integration of heterogeneous data sources offers unprecedented opportunities to improve clinical decision-making. Electronic Health Records (EHR) contain complementary information -- including narrative clinical reports, numerical measurements, and structured variables -- yet most survival models remain limited to a single modality or fail to exploit the temporal nature of patient trajectories. We propose MultiSigBERT, a unified framework for multimodal sequential survival modeling in oncology based on path signature representations. Here, narrative medical reports (free-text) are converted into sentence embeddings by extracting and averaging contextual word embeddings. These representations are then compressed via modality-specific PCA and concatenated with structured covariates to form joint temporal trajectories which are then encoded using the Signature transform, a tool from Rough Paths theory that efficiently captures higher-order temporal interactions across modalities without supervision needed. The computed Signature features are finally incorporated as high dimensional features into a LASSO-regularized Cox model to estimate individualized risk scores. The performance of our novel MultiSigBERT pipeline is illustrated on the analysis of a real-world oncology cohort from the Léon Bérard Center, comprising over 120,000 medical reports and structured records from more than 2,500 patients. The model achieves a concordance index of 0.743 (sd 0.029) on an independent test set, demonstrating the benefit of jointly modeling multimodal temporal dynamics together with patient-level geometric structure for survival prediction.

</details>


### [103] [Position: Fairness Failure in Generative Models is an Evaluation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16974)
*Mariia Vladimirova,Jean-Yves Franceschi,Thibaut Issenhuth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出生成模型中的公平性问题本质上源于评估困境：不同论文间的公平性发现难以比较、难以部署决策。作者提出"公平卡"（Fairness Cards）作为标准化报告工件，明确记录提示族、反事实协议、指标和拒答处理等评估选择，以提升可复现性、可比性和可问责性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成模型在过去十年取得突破性进展，但其公平性缺失、强化社会不平等和伤害边缘群体的问题仍未得到充分解决且难以采取行动。作者认为，公平性失败虽然由多重因素驱动，但归根结底是一个评估问题——公平性发现很少在论文之间具有可比性，也难以用于部署决策。

Method: 作者首先诊断当前实践中的反复出现的经验性和概念性失败模式，然后提出范式转变：从临时性偏见检查转向标准化、生成模型专属的评估。核心贡献是提出"公平卡"（Fairness Cards）作为最小化报告工件，使评估选择显式化——包括提示族（prompt families）、反事实协议（counterfactual protocols）、指标（metrics）和拒答处理（refusal handling），从而确保可复现性、可比性和可问责性。

Result: 论文通过诊断分析指出了公平性评估领域存在的系统性问题，并提出Fairness Cards这一具体报告框架作为解决方案。虽然本文是立场论文而非实证研究，但其贡献在于提供了一个可操作的标准化工件模板，并附带公开的项目页面。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁从临时的、碎片化的偏见检查转向生成模型专属的标准化评估范式，Fairness Cards作为最小报告工件是这一转变的核心抓手。论文最后还提出了推动评估标准范式转变的额外建议，旨在实现更加可复现、可比较和可问责的公平性评估。

Abstract: Despite groundbreaking advancements in generative models during the last decade, concerns about their lack of fairness, reinforcing societal inequalities and harming marginalized groups, remain under-addressed and difficult to act upon. This position paper argues that fairness failures in generative models, albeit driven by multiple factors, are ultimately stemming from an evaluation problem: fairness findings are rarely comparable across papers or actionable for deployment decisions. This paper diagnoses recurring empirical and conceptual failure modes in current practice and motivates a shift from ad-hoc bias checks to standardized, generative-specific evaluation. We propose Fairness Cards as a minimal reporting artifact that makes evaluation choices explicit (prompt families, counterfactual protocols, metrics, and refusal handling) enabling reproducibility, comparability, and accountability. We conclude with additional recommendations towards a paradigm shift in evaluation standards. Our project page can be found at https://mariiavladimirova.github.io/fairness-cards .

</details>


### [104] [Agents unlock new capabilities through Switching LoRA Adapters as a Tool (SLAaaT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17034)
*Kenneth Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种让智能体在代理轨迹中途自主切换不同LoRA适配器的工具，以解决后训练导致的灾难性遗忘问题，从而避免在多能力组合任务中牺牲某些领域的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练虽然能解锁新能力并提升特定任务表现，但常常导致其他领域的灾难性遗忘。在需要组合多种能力的长代理轨迹中，这种权衡成为一个关键问题。

Method: 给代理一个工具，使其能够在轨迹中途切换不同的专用LoRA适配器（Low-Rank Adaptation），并通过两个综合的合成编码任务来测试该方法的有效性。这些任务逻辑简单但需要专业化能力。

Result: 模型能够解决之前无法解决的问题；能够自主切换适配器，并在一个任务上找到了优于人工启发式基线的新策略；相比仅使用单一专用适配器的代理，能力代价最多降低了18倍；在任务能力和token使用上均显著优于生成子代理的方法。

Conclusion: 通过为代理提供中途切换专用LoRA适配器的工具，可以有效消除后训练带来的能力权衡，在多能力组合任务中同时保留各领域专长，且优于子代理方案。

Abstract: Post-training can unlock new capabilities and improve performance on specialized tasks, but sometimes at the cost of catastrophic forgetting in other domains. This poses a problem in long agent trajectories that compose different capabilities. We reject this tradeoff by giving an agent a tool to switch between specialized LoRA adapters mid-trace. To test its effectiveness, we compose two synthetic coding tasks that are logically simple but require specialization. We find that this allows the model to solve problems it previously could not, that the model is able to switch autonomously (and find a new strategy that beats our human heuristic baseline on one task), and that this incurs an up to an 18x reduction in capability tax compared to an agent using only one specialized adapter. Our approach also substantially outperforms spawning subagents in both task capabilities and token usage.

</details>


### [105] [J-Miner: Recovering Executable Decision Knowledge from Language-Model Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17063)
*Yunfan Gao,Xinyi Huang,Tao Sheng,Haorui Song,Yun Xiong,Haofen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出J-Miner方法，从微调后的语言模型中挖掘内部决策知识，将其编码为可检查、可验证、可复用的显式可执行规则，在多个分类任务上重现高达98.3%的源分类器决策，并将知识迁移到参数仅约1/24的轻量级学生模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
